> - Postflop: value-bet top pair+ and two pair; bet top pair good kicker when checked to. Bottom or second pair: check, fold to any real bet unless 5:1+ and heads-up.
> - Draws: stop calling multiway draws with multiple bets on turn/river. Only chase when 2+ pot odds are clear and heads-up.
> - Respect turn and river bets from anyone who bet earlier; I lost chips calling pairs into flush/straight boards.
